\section{9. Power Simulation (dynamic --- significant outcomes
only)}\label{power-simulation-dynamic-significant-outcomes-only}

Design-based power simulation ported verbatim from the original file:
synthetic treated firms are drawn from the matched controls reproducing
the real cohort sizes, a constant additive effect is imposed on the log
outcome over the post-period, the C\&S overall ATT is re-estimated per
draw, and the rejection rate at the 5\% level is recorded. The
methodology and the per-effect-size draw count are unchanged. The only
change: it runs \textbf{only on the outcomes flagged as findings} in
section 4 (\texttt{p\_value\ \textless{}\ SIG\_THRESHOLD}), mapped back
to their \texttt{outcome\_map} keys, and the combined plot shows just
those, each with a vertical line at its estimated ATT.

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\NormalTok{run\_power\_sim }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \ControlFlowTok{function}\NormalTok{(cs\_data, outcome\_var, att\_true,}
                          \AttributeTok{n\_sims =} \DecValTok{100}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{label =} \StringTok{""}\NormalTok{) \{}
\NormalTok{  controls\_df   }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ cs\_data[cs\_data}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{is\_control }\SpecialCharTok{==} \DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{, ]}
\NormalTok{  control\_names }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{unique}\NormalTok{(controls\_df}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{Linkedin\_name)}

\NormalTok{  real\_cohorts }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ cs\_data }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{filter}\NormalTok{(is\_treated }\SpecialCharTok{==} \DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{distinct}\NormalTok{(Linkedin\_name, g\_period) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{count}\NormalTok{(g\_period) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{arrange}\NormalTok{(g\_period)}

\NormalTok{  results }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{numeric}\NormalTok{(n\_sims)}

  \ControlFlowTok{for}\NormalTok{ (i }\ControlFlowTok{in} \FunctionTok{seq\_len}\NormalTok{(n\_sims)) \{}
\NormalTok{    available    }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ control\_names}
\NormalTok{    treated\_pool }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{character}\NormalTok{(}\DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{    g\_vec        }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{integer}\NormalTok{(}\DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{)}

    \ControlFlowTok{for}\NormalTok{ (k }\ControlFlowTok{in} \FunctionTok{seq\_len}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{nrow}\NormalTok{(real\_cohorts))) \{}
\NormalTok{      n\_k    }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ real\_cohorts}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{n[k]}
\NormalTok{      cohort }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ real\_cohorts}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{g\_period[k]}
      \ControlFlowTok{if}\NormalTok{ (}\FunctionTok{length}\NormalTok{(available) }\SpecialCharTok{\textless{}}\NormalTok{ n\_k) }\ControlFlowTok{break}
\NormalTok{      sampled      }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{sample}\NormalTok{(available, n\_k, }\AttributeTok{replace =} \ConstantTok{FALSE}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{      available    }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{setdiff}\NormalTok{(available, sampled)}
\NormalTok{      treated\_pool }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(treated\_pool, sampled)}
\NormalTok{      g\_vec        }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(g\_vec, }\FunctionTok{rep}\NormalTok{(cohort, n\_k))}
\NormalTok{    \}}

\NormalTok{    g\_lookup }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{data.frame}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{Linkedin\_name =}\NormalTok{ treated\_pool,}
                           \AttributeTok{g\_sim =}\NormalTok{ g\_vec,}
                           \AttributeTok{stringsAsFactors =} \ConstantTok{FALSE}\NormalTok{)}

\NormalTok{    sim\_treated }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ controls\_df }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
      \FunctionTok{filter}\NormalTok{(Linkedin\_name }\SpecialCharTok{\%in\%}\NormalTok{ treated\_pool) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
      \FunctionTok{left\_join}\NormalTok{(g\_lookup, }\AttributeTok{by =} \StringTok{"Linkedin\_name"}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
      \FunctionTok{mutate}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{post\_sim     =} \FunctionTok{as.integer}\NormalTok{(period }\SpecialCharTok{\textgreater{}=}\NormalTok{ g\_sim),}
             \AttributeTok{outcome\_sim  =}\NormalTok{ .data[[outcome\_var]] }\SpecialCharTok{+}\NormalTok{ att\_true }\SpecialCharTok{*}\NormalTok{ post\_sim,}
             \AttributeTok{g\_period\_sim =}\NormalTok{ g\_sim)}

\NormalTok{    sim\_controls }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ controls\_df }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
      \FunctionTok{filter}\NormalTok{(Linkedin\_name }\SpecialCharTok{\%in\%}\NormalTok{ available) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
      \FunctionTok{mutate}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{outcome\_sim  =}\NormalTok{ .data[[outcome\_var]],}
             \AttributeTok{g\_period\_sim =} \DecValTok{0}\DataTypeTok{L}\NormalTok{)}

\NormalTok{    sim\_data }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{bind\_rows}\NormalTok{(sim\_treated, sim\_controls) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
      \FunctionTok{mutate}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{firm\_id\_sim =} \FunctionTok{as.integer}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{factor}\NormalTok{(Linkedin\_name)))}

\NormalTok{    result }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{tryCatch}\NormalTok{(\{}
\NormalTok{      cs\_sim }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{att\_gt}\NormalTok{(}
        \AttributeTok{yname         =} \StringTok{"outcome\_sim"}\NormalTok{,}
        \AttributeTok{tname         =} \StringTok{"period"}\NormalTok{,}
        \AttributeTok{idname        =} \StringTok{"firm\_id\_sim"}\NormalTok{,}
        \AttributeTok{gname         =} \StringTok{"g\_period\_sim"}\NormalTok{,}
        \AttributeTok{control\_group =} \StringTok{"notyettreated"}\NormalTok{,}
        \AttributeTok{anticipation  =} \DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{,   }\CommentTok{\# paper assumption: no anticipation}
        \AttributeTok{bstrap        =} \ConstantTok{FALSE}\NormalTok{,}
        \AttributeTok{panel         =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{,}
        \AttributeTok{print\_details =} \ConstantTok{FALSE}\NormalTok{,}
        \AttributeTok{data          =}\NormalTok{ sim\_data}
\NormalTok{      )}
\NormalTok{      agg\_sim }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{aggte}\NormalTok{(cs\_sim, }\AttributeTok{type =} \StringTok{"simple"}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{      z\_stat  }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ agg\_sim}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{overall.att }\SpecialCharTok{/}\NormalTok{ agg\_sim}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{overall.se}
      \FunctionTok{as.integer}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{abs}\NormalTok{(z\_stat) }\SpecialCharTok{\textgreater{}} \FloatTok{1.96}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{    \}, }\AttributeTok{error =} \ControlFlowTok{function}\NormalTok{(e) }\ConstantTok{NA\_integer\_}\NormalTok{)}

\NormalTok{    results[i] }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ result}
\NormalTok{  \}}

  \FunctionTok{mean}\NormalTok{(results, }\AttributeTok{na.rm =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{\}}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# Map section{-}4 display names back to outcome\_map keys.}
\CommentTok{\# (SIG\_THRESHOLD comes from the setup chunk.)}
\NormalTok{key\_by\_outcome }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}
  \StringTok{"Technology{-}tagged collaborations"}    \OtherTok{=} \StringTok{"tech\_focused"}\NormalTok{,}
  \StringTok{"Cross{-}border EU collaborations"}      \OtherTok{=} \StringTok{"cross\_border"}\NormalTok{,}
  \StringTok{"Cross{-}sector collaborations"}         \OtherTok{=} \StringTok{"cross\_sector"}\NormalTok{,}
  \StringTok{"Cross{-}sector EU{-}only collaborations"} \OtherTok{=} \StringTok{"cross\_sector\_eu"}\NormalTok{,}
  \StringTok{"Non{-}EU collaborations"}               \OtherTok{=} \StringTok{"non\_eu"}\NormalTok{,}
  \StringTok{"Domestic collaborations"}             \OtherTok{=} \StringTok{"domestic"}\NormalTok{,}
  \StringTok{"Within{-}sector collaborations"}        \OtherTok{=} \StringTok{"within\_sector"}\NormalTok{,}
  \StringTok{"Non{-}funded collaborations"}           \OtherTok{=} \StringTok{"non\_funded"}
\NormalTok{)}

\NormalTok{sig\_rows }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ att\_table[}\SpecialCharTok{!}\FunctionTok{is.na}\NormalTok{(att\_table}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{p\_value) }\SpecialCharTok{\&}
\NormalTok{                        att\_table}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{p\_value }\SpecialCharTok{\textless{}}\NormalTok{ SIG\_THRESHOLD, , drop }\OtherTok{=} \ConstantTok{FALSE}\NormalTok{]}
\NormalTok{sig\_rows}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{key }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ key\_by\_outcome[sig\_rows}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{Outcome]}
\NormalTok{sig\_rows }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ sig\_rows[}\SpecialCharTok{!}\FunctionTok{is.na}\NormalTok{(sig\_rows}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{key), , drop }\OtherTok{=} \ConstantTok{FALSE}\NormalTok{]}

\FunctionTok{cat}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"}\SpecialCharTok{\textbackslash{}n}\StringTok{=== Significant outcomes (p \textless{}"}\NormalTok{, SIG\_THRESHOLD, }\StringTok{","}\NormalTok{, TIME\_UNIT, }\StringTok{") ===}\SpecialCharTok{\textbackslash{}n}\StringTok{"}\NormalTok{)}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\end{verbatim}

\ControlFlowTok{if}\NormalTok{ (}\FunctionTok{nrow}\NormalTok{(sig\_rows) }\SpecialCharTok{==} \DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{) \{}
  \FunctionTok{cat}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"  none {-}{-} power simulation will be skipped.}\SpecialCharTok{\textbackslash{}n}\StringTok{"}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{\} }\ControlFlowTok{else}\NormalTok{ \{}
  \FunctionTok{print}\NormalTok{(sig\_rows[, }\FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"Technology"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"Outcome"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"ATT"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"p\_value"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"key"}\NormalTok{)],}

\end{verbatim}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\FunctionTok{library}\NormalTok{(parallel)}

\CommentTok{\# Robust parallelism: fork when available, but fall back to serial if the fork}
\CommentTok{\# workers fail (e.g. a non{-}interactive session), so an overnight run never}
\CommentTok{\# silently returns no results. Shadows parallel::mclapply for the calls below.}
\NormalTok{mclapply }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \ControlFlowTok{function}\NormalTok{(X, FUN, ..., }\AttributeTok{mc.cores =} \FunctionTok{min}\NormalTok{(}\DecValTok{4}\DataTypeTok{L}\NormalTok{, parallel}\SpecialCharTok{::}\FunctionTok{detectCores}\NormalTok{())) \{}
\NormalTok{  res }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{tryCatch}\NormalTok{(parallel}\SpecialCharTok{::}\FunctionTok{mclapply}\NormalTok{(X, FUN, ..., }\AttributeTok{mc.cores =}\NormalTok{ mc.cores),}
                  \AttributeTok{error =} \ControlFlowTok{function}\NormalTok{(e) base}\SpecialCharTok{::}\FunctionTok{lapply}\NormalTok{(X, FUN))}
  \ControlFlowTok{if}\NormalTok{ (}\FunctionTok{any}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{vapply}\NormalTok{(res, }\ControlFlowTok{function}\NormalTok{(z) }\FunctionTok{inherits}\NormalTok{(z, }\StringTok{"try{-}error"}\NormalTok{), }\FunctionTok{logical}\NormalTok{(}\DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{))))}
\NormalTok{    res }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ base}\SpecialCharTok{::}\FunctionTok{lapply}\NormalTok{(X, FUN)}
\NormalTok{  res}
\NormalTok{\}}

\NormalTok{att\_grid     }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{log}\NormalTok{(}\DecValTok{1} \SpecialCharTok{+} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\FloatTok{0.10}\NormalTok{, }\FloatTok{0.20}\NormalTok{, }\FloatTok{0.30}\NormalTok{))}
\NormalTok{att\_pct\_grid }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{round}\NormalTok{((}\FunctionTok{exp}\NormalTok{(att\_grid) }\SpecialCharTok{{-}} \DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{*} \DecValTok{100}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{)}

\ControlFlowTok{if}\NormalTok{ (}\FunctionTok{nrow}\NormalTok{(sig\_rows) }\SpecialCharTok{==} \DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{) \{}
  \FunctionTok{cat}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"No significant outcomes {-}{-} skipping power simulation.}\SpecialCharTok{\textbackslash{}n}\StringTok{"}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{  power\_long }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \ConstantTok{NULL}
\NormalTok{\} }\ControlFlowTok{else}\NormalTok{ \{}
\NormalTok{  power\_rows }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{list}\NormalTok{()}
  \ControlFlowTok{for}\NormalTok{ (i }\ControlFlowTok{in} \FunctionTok{seq\_len}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{nrow}\NormalTok{(sig\_rows))) \{}
\NormalTok{    tech  }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ sig\_rows}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{Technology[i]}
\NormalTok{    key   }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ sig\_rows}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{key[i]}
\NormalTok{    oc    }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ sig\_rows}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{Outcome[i]}
\NormalTok{    dfp   }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \ControlFlowTok{if}\NormalTok{ (tech }\SpecialCharTok{==} \StringTok{"Battery"}\NormalTok{) battery\_s1 }\ControlFlowTok{else}\NormalTok{ hydrogen\_s1}
\NormalTok{    yname }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ outcome\_map[[key]][}\FunctionTok{tolower}\NormalTok{(tech)]}
\NormalTok{    lbl   }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{paste}\NormalTok{(tech, }\StringTok{"{-}{-}"}\NormalTok{, oc)}

    \FunctionTok{cat}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"Power simulation:"}\NormalTok{, lbl, }\StringTok{"("}\NormalTok{, yname, }\StringTok{")}\SpecialCharTok{\textbackslash{}n}\StringTok{"}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{    pw }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{unlist}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{mclapply}\NormalTok{(att\_grid, }\ControlFlowTok{function}\NormalTok{(a)}
      \FunctionTok{run\_power\_sim}\NormalTok{(dfp, yname, a, }\DecValTok{100}\NormalTok{, lbl), }\AttributeTok{mc.cores =} \DecValTok{4}\NormalTok{))}

\NormalTok{    power\_rows[[}\FunctionTok{length}\NormalTok{(power\_rows) }\SpecialCharTok{+} \DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{]] }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{data.frame}\NormalTok{(}
      \AttributeTok{spec    =}\NormalTok{ lbl,}
      \AttributeTok{att\_pct =}\NormalTok{ att\_pct\_grid,}
      \AttributeTok{power   =}\NormalTok{ pw,}
      \AttributeTok{stringsAsFactors =} \ConstantTok{FALSE}
\NormalTok{    )}
\NormalTok{  \}}
\NormalTok{  power\_long }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{do.call}\NormalTok{(rbind, power\_rows)}
  \FunctionTok{print}\NormalTok{(power\_long)}
  \FunctionTok{write.csv}\NormalTok{(power\_long,}
            \FunctionTok{file.path}\NormalTok{(out\_dir, }\StringTok{"power\_techtagged.csv"}\NormalTok{), }\AttributeTok{row.names =} \ConstantTok{FALSE}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{\}}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]

\section{10. Consortium size (mechanism
check)}\label{consortium-size-mechanism-check}

Did treated firms join LARGER multi-firm collaborations after approval?
This is a potential mechanical driver of the composition results.
\texttt{mean\_collab\_size} is the mean number of firms per
collaboration a firm participates in, per period --- a \textbf{level},
not a count and not a share. It is run on the RAW column: no
\texttt{log1p}, not in \texttt{outcome\_map}. Technology-specific
control group via \texttt{cg\_for()} (battery \texttt{nevertreated},
hydrogen \texttt{notyettreated}); \texttt{anticipation\ =\ 0} is already
set inside \texttt{run\_cs()}. \texttt{TIME\_UNIT}-aware (quarterly
averages the per-month means).

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# Two versions, see the note above:}
\CommentTok{\#   mean\_collab\_size\_active {-}{-} NA in firm{-}months without a collaboration, so C\&S}
\CommentTok{\#     drops them and the ATT is INTENSIVE margin. This is the headline mechanism.}
\CommentTok{\#   mean\_collab\_size        {-}{-} zero{-}filled, mixes extensive and intensive margins.}
\CommentTok{\#     Reported alongside so the difference between the two is visible.}
\NormalTok{SIZE\_COLS }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{active =} \StringTok{"mean\_collab\_size\_active"}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{allmonths =} \StringTok{"mean\_collab\_size"}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{SIZE\_LAB  }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{active =} \StringTok{"Consortium size (active months)"}\NormalTok{,}
               \AttributeTok{allmonths =} \StringTok{"Consortium size (incl. zero{-}collaboration months)"}\NormalTok{)}

\ControlFlowTok{if}\NormalTok{ (RUN\_CONSORTIUM }\SpecialCharTok{\&\&} \StringTok{"mean\_collab\_size"} \SpecialCharTok{\%in\%} \FunctionTok{names}\NormalTok{(battery\_s1)) \{}
\NormalTok{  cs\_size }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{list}\NormalTok{(); size\_rows }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{list}\NormalTok{()}
  \ControlFlowTok{for}\NormalTok{ (v }\ControlFlowTok{in} \FunctionTok{names}\NormalTok{(SIZE\_COLS)) \{}
\NormalTok{    col }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ SIZE\_COLS[[v]]}
    \ControlFlowTok{if}\NormalTok{ (}\SpecialCharTok{!}\NormalTok{(col }\SpecialCharTok{\%in\%} \FunctionTok{names}\NormalTok{(battery\_s1))) \{}
      \FunctionTok{cat}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"   "}\NormalTok{, col, }\StringTok{"not in panel {-}{-} re{-}run 05a. Skipping.}\SpecialCharTok{\textbackslash{}n}\StringTok{"}\NormalTok{); }\ControlFlowTok{next}
\NormalTok{    \}}
\NormalTok{    cs\_size[[v]] }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{list}\NormalTok{(}
      \AttributeTok{battery  =} \FunctionTok{run\_cs}\NormalTok{(battery\_s1,  col,}
                        \FunctionTok{paste}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"Battery {-}{-}"}\NormalTok{, SIZE\_LAB[[v]]),}
                        \AttributeTok{control\_group =} \FunctionTok{cg\_for}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"battery"}\NormalTok{)),}
      \AttributeTok{hydrogen =} \FunctionTok{run\_cs}\NormalTok{(hydrogen\_s1, col,}
                        \FunctionTok{paste}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"Hydrogen {-}{-}"}\NormalTok{, SIZE\_LAB[[v]]),}
                        \AttributeTok{control\_group =} \FunctionTok{cg\_for}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"hydrogen"}\NormalTok{))}
\NormalTok{    )}
\NormalTok{    size\_rows[[v]] }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{do.call}\NormalTok{(rbind, }\FunctionTok{list}\NormalTok{(}
      \FunctionTok{make\_att\_row}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{agg\_simple}\NormalTok{(cs\_size[[v]]}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{battery),  }\StringTok{"Battery"}\NormalTok{,}
\NormalTok{                   SIZE\_LAB[[v]], }\StringTok{"Mechanism"}\NormalTok{,}
\NormalTok{                   cs\_size[[v]]}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{battery,  battery\_s1,  col),}

\NormalTok{                   SIZE\_LAB[[v]], }\StringTok{"Mechanism"}\NormalTok{,}

\NormalTok{    ))}
\NormalTok{  \}}

\NormalTok{  cs\_size }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \ControlFlowTok{if}\NormalTok{ (}\SpecialCharTok{!}\FunctionTok{is.null}\NormalTok{(cs\_size}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{active)) cs\_size}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{active }\ControlFlowTok{else}\NormalTok{ cs\_size}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{allmonths}
  \FunctionTok{cat}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"}\SpecialCharTok{\textbackslash{}n}\StringTok{=== Consortium size mechanism check ("}\NormalTok{, TIME\_UNIT, }\StringTok{") ===}\SpecialCharTok{\textbackslash{}n}\StringTok{"}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{sep =} \StringTok{""}\NormalTok{)}

\section{Notes for carrying this into your existing sections
5--10}\label{notes-for-carrying-this-into-your-existing-sections-510}

Your downstream sections need only a \textbf{column-name swap} (and they
inherit the contiguous index + quarterly toggle for free, because they
all reuse the data frames and \texttt{run\_cs()} defined above):

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \textbf{Robustness S1 / clustering / never-treated:} replace each
  \texttt{yname\ =\ "..."} with the tech-tagged column from
  \texttt{outcome\_map}, e.g.
  \texttt{outcome\_map\$cross\_border{[}"hydrogen"{]}}.
\item
  \textbf{Power simulation:} point \texttt{outcome\_var} at the
  tech-tagged column; the zero share is higher, so expect the minimum
  detectable effect to rise --- this is exactly the quantity to report
  when justifying the quarterly check.
\item
  \textbf{Rosenbaum bounds:} unchanged mechanics; just feed the
  tech-tagged count.
\item
  \textbf{Export for Python:} dynamic exports use the corrected
  event-time index now, so your \texttt{08\_ck\_visualisation-results}
  event studies will be right at year boundaries.
\end{itemize}
